Q1. After a tank becomes full, the pipe continues to run at 12 litres per minute for 6 minutes. Find the wasted water.
A. 70 litres
B. 86.4 litres
C. 77 litres
D. 72 litres
Answer: 72 litres
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_overflow_waste_rate
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
Waste=r\times t
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
Waste=12\times 6
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
Waste=72
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=72
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Waste=extra\ inflow
\]
Answer: 72 litres

---

Q2. 720 person-days of food are available. 30 people consume it for 8 days, then the group becomes 40. Find how many more days the food will last.
A. 12 days
B. 9 days
C. 11 days
D. 17 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_food_population_change
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
D=\frac{720-30\times 8}{40}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
D=12
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=remaining\ stock/final\ people
\]
Answer: 12 days

---

Q4. A filling pipe adds 12 units per hour and an emptying pipe removes 5 units in the next hour alternately. Tank capacity is 60 units. Find the filling time.
A. 11.33 hours
B. 19.33 hours
C. 12.25 hours
D. 16.33 hours
Answer: 16.33 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: pc_alternating_fill_empty
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W_{cycle}=12-5
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
60\ units,\ cycle=7
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=16.33
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=16.33
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=full\ cycles+terminal\ fill
\]
Answer: 16.33 hours

Q5. A and B work in alternate hours, starting with A. Their hourly outputs are 10 and 4 units. Find the time for 90 units.
A. 10.08 hours
B. 18.6 hours
C. 9.6 hours
D. 12.6 hours
Answer: 12.6 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_hours
Explanation:

\[

\]

\[
W_{cycle}=10+4
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=12.6
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=12.6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 12.6 hours

Q9. Pair times are AB=10 days, BC=12 days and CA=15 days. Find the time taken by A alone.
A. 25 days
B. 12 days
C. 30 days
D. 24 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_pairwise_ab_bc_ac
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
2(A+B+C)=(A+B)+(B+C)+(C+A)
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
AB=10,\ BC=12,\ CA=15
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T_A=24
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
A=\frac{AB+AC-BC}{2}
\]
Answer: 24 days

---

Q10. 8 workers have completed 48 units of a 300-unit job. Only 8 days remain. How many extra workers are needed?
A. 24 workers
B. 20 workers
C. 28.8 workers
D. 26 workers
Answer: 24 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
extra=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-current
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
extra=\left\lceil\frac{252}{1\times 8}\right\rceil-8
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
extra=24
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
extra=required-current
\]
Answer: 24 workers

Q13. 16 men working 6 hours a day finish a work in 12 days. How many men are needed to finish it in 20 days working 5 hours a day?
A. 26.67 workers
B. 11.52 workers
C. 15 workers
D. 16 workers
Answer: 11.52 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_farm_harvest_workers
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
16\times 12\times 6=M_2\times 20\times 5
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
M_2=11.52
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=11.52
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{16\times 12\times 6}{20\times 5}=11.52
\]
Answer: 11.52 workers

---

Q14. Pipe A fills a tank in 12 hours. Pipes A and B together fill it in 8 hours. Find the time taken by B alone.
A. 12 hours
B. 30 hours
C. 22 hours
D. 24 hours
Answer: 24 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{8}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
B=24
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 24 hours

B. 9 days

\[

\[

\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
B=12
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{6\times 12}{12-6}=12
\]
Answer: 12 days

\]

\[
Accepted=408
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.

Ans=408
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Accepted=gross\times p/100
\]
Answer: 408 pages

Q23. A team can complete a work in 12 days. It works for 10 days. What fraction of work is left?
A. 5/6
B. 1/5
C. 1/6
D. 6/1
Answer: 1/6
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_residual_work
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
R=1-\frac{10}{12}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
R=\frac{2}{12}=1/6
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=1/6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{12-10}{12}=1/6
\]
Answer: 1/6

Q28. A job changes team after 3 days. A, B and C alone take 8, 18 and 24 days. If B is replaced by C, find the completion time.

D. 4.6 days
Answer: 5.75 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_replacement_worker
Explanation:

\[

\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=13\times 3
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=3+\frac{33}{12}=5.75
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=5.75
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 5.75 days

Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[

\]

\[

\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T_A:T_B=13:10
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=13:10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=13:10
\]
Answer: 13:10

Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=3\times 2,\quad R=90-6
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=2+\frac{84}{3+5}=12.5
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=12.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=2+\frac{90-3\times 2}{3+5}
\]
Answer: 12.5 days

Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
\sum r_it_i=W
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=15\times 2
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=2+\frac{90}{26}=5.46
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=5.46
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 5.46 days

\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
T=\frac{150}{18-4}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=10.71
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=10.71
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=W/net\ rate
\]
Answer: 10.71 days

Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_{cycle}=6+6
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=10
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 10 hours

Q491. A job has 300 units. 8 workers finish 80 units, and 10 days are left. Find the extra workers required.

D. 10 workers
Answer: 14 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:

\[
extra=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-current
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
extra=\left\lceil\frac{220}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-8
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
extra=14
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=14
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
extra=required-current
\]
Answer: 14 workers

Q493. A and B together complete a work in 12 days. A alone takes 15 days. Find the time taken by B alone.

B. 3 days
C. 13.5 days

Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:

\[

\]

\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{15}
\]

\[
B=60
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.

Ans=60
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{12\times 15}{15-12}=60
\]
Answer: 60 days

Q498. A job changes team after 3 days. A, B and C alone take 24, 10 and 16 days. If B is replaced by C, find the completion time.

C. 6.52 days
D. 3.52 days
Answer: 8.52 days

Family: tw_replacement_worker
Explanation:

\[

\]

\[

\]

\[

\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.

Ans=8.52
\]

\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 8.52 days

Q500. 720 person-days of food are available. 20 people consume it for 6 days, then the group becomes 25. Find how many more days the food will last.
A. 36 days
B. 28 days
C. 24 days
D. 26 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_food_remaining_stock
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
D=\frac{720-20\times 6}{25}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
D=24
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=remaining\ stock/final\ people
\]
Answer: 24 days
